### [1] [Nontrivial solutions for a class of semilinear elliptic equations with a nonlinear Goldstein-Wentzell boundary condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28709)
*Enzo Vitillaro*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有耦合边界条件的双重椭圆问题的非平凡解的存在性与多重性，证明了在势阱能量水平（非平凡解的最小能量水平）下问题存在非平凡解，并在更高能量水平下存在无穷多个解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在解决带混合边界条件（Dirichlet边界与边界上椭圆方程耦合）的双重椭圆问题。这类问题涉及体区域与边界之间的耦合非线性，具有重要的物理和几何意义。作者希望确定非平凡解的存在条件以及解的数量特征，特别是在能量水平维度上的刻画。

Method: 论文采用变分方法，将问题转化为能量泛函的临界点问题。利用Sobolev嵌入中f和g的次临界条件，通过势井方法建立解的存在性，并基于对称性（可能结合山路引理或喷泉定理）证明更高能量水平下无穷多个解的存在性。

Result: 在适当假设下，证明了问题在势阱能量深度处存在非平凡解，该能量水平即为非平凡解的最小能量水平；同时证明了在更高能量水平上问题存在无穷多个解。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了此类双重椭圆耦合边界问题的解的存在性与多重性结构：最低非平凡解出现在势阱能量水平，且更高能量水平上解具有无穷多重性。

Abstract: The paper deals with the existence and multiplicity of nontrivial solutions for the doubly elliptic problem $$\begin{cases} -Δu=f(u) \qquad &\text{in $Ω$,}\\ \phantom{-}u=0 &\text{on $Γ_0$,}\\ -Δ_Γu +\partial_νu =g(u)\qquad &\text{on $Γ_1$,} \end{cases} $$ where $Ω$ is a bounded open domain of $\mathbb{R}^N$ ($N\ge 2$) with $C^1$ boundary $Γ=\partialΩ$, with $Γ=Γ_0\cupΓ_1$, $Γ_0\capΓ_1=\emptyset$, $Γ_1$ being nonempty and relatively open on $Γ$, $\mathcal{H}^{N-1}(Γ_0)>0$. The terms $f$ and $g$ are subcritical with respect to Sobolev embeddings, respectively in $Ω$ and on $\partialΩ$.
  We prove that, under suitable assumptions, the problem admits nontrivial solutions at the depth of the potential well energy level, which is the minimum energy level for nontrivial solutions. We also prove that the problem has infinitely many solutions at higher energy levels.

</details>


### [2] [Some remarks on structured Keyfitz-Kranzer systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28860)
*Ralph Saxton,Katarzyna Saxton*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对形式为 U_t + (Φ(U)U)_x = 0 的守恒律系统，通过施加结构条件，基于 Φ(U) = φ(r, Θ) 的形式对解进行分类。通过规定特定特征值的演化方式，将此类函数 φ 分为依赖于标量场 z = rK(Θ) 或向量场 Θ 两类，并确定解的振幅何时会在有限时间内爆破。相应地，对应的黎曼问题的解可分为经典解和涉及δ-激波和/或真空状态的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为守恒律系统提供一种基于函数 φ(U) 形式的分类框架，从而统一理解不同结构条件下解的演化行为，特别是有限时间爆破与黎曼问题中可能出现的奇异行为（δ-激波和真空态）。

Method: 通过对守恒律系统的通量函数施加结构条件，规定某个特定特征值的演化方式来辅助分类，将 φ 的类别根据其依赖性划分为标量场型 z = rK(Θ) 和向量场型 Θ 两大类，并分析相应的解行为。

Result: 明确了在哪些 φ 类别下解的振幅会在有限时间内爆破；将黎曼问题的解划分为经典解以及涉及δ-激波和/或真空状态的解两大类。

Conclusion: 该分类框架为这类守恒律系统的解行为提供了系统性认识，能够依据通量函数的形态预测解的演化特征（是否爆破）以及黎曼问题解的类型（经典或奇异解）。

Abstract: Several applications for systems of conservation laws of the form $U_t + (Φ(U) U)_x =0$, $U: R_t\times R_x\rightarrow R^n$ , $n\geq 2$, with $Φ(U) = φ(r, Θ): R^n\rightarrow R$, $r = |U|$, and $Θ= U/|U|\in S^{n-1}$, are obtained by imposing structural conditions to provide a classification framework for solutions dependent on the form of $φ(U)$. By prescribing the evolution of a particular eigenvalue, we can categorize classes of such functions, $φ$, for which this evolution is met, into depending on either a scalar field $z=rK(Θ)$, where $K: S^{n-1}\rightarrow R$, or on the vector field $Θ$, and find for which the amplitude of solutions may blow up in finite time. As a consequence, solutions to the corresponding Riemann problems can be divided into those with are classical and those which involve delta-shocks and/or vacuum states.

</details>


### [3] [Recovery of a Null Form in the Wave Equation from Scattering Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28917)
*Joel Nathe*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用高频几何光学（HGOS）解来求解一类带有对称和反对称零形式（null forms）的非线性波动方程系统的逆问题，分别在线性和弱非线性区域讨论了系数确定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有非线性零形式耦合项的多分量波动方程系统，需要发展逆散射/逆问题方法，以从边界或远场测量中恢复方程中的未知系数（$q_{rkl}$ 和 $p_{rijkl}$）。

Method: 采用高频几何光学（highly oscillatory geometric optics）解的构造方法。在线性区域，利用 $h^3$ 阶系数定义一个向量场的光线变换（light-ray transform）并证明其单射性；在弱非线性区域，利用 $h$ 阶系数构造矩阵值的光线变换（非阿贝尔情形），并讨论其在系数不依赖于时间变量 $x_0$ 时退化为非阿贝尔X射线变换的可注入性结果。

Result: 在线性区域，$h^3$ 阶系数可确定一个依赖 $q_{rkl}, p_{rijkl}$ 的向量场的单射光线变换。在弱非线性区域，$h$ 阶系数确定与系数相关的矩阵的非阿贝尔光线变换；在系数与时间无关的情况下，该变换简化为单射的非阿贝尔X射线变换。

Conclusion: 本文给出了该非线性波动方程系统在线性和弱非线性两种区域下的逆问题求解方案。线性情形取得了完整的可注入性结果，弱非线性情形在一般时变设定下尚缺少可注入性结论，但在静态（与时间无关）情形下通过非阿贝尔X射线变换获得可注入性。

Abstract: We use highly oscillatory geometric optics solutions to solve the inverse problem for the system \begin{equation} \square \begin{bmatrix} u^{(1)}\\ u^{(2)}\\ \vdots\\ u^{(n)}\end{bmatrix} = \sum_{\substack{k,l=1\\k\geq l}}^n \left(Q_0(u^{(k)},u^{(l)}) \begin{bmatrix} q_{1kl}\\q_{2kl}\\\vdots\\q_{nkl} \end{bmatrix} + \sum_{\substack{i,j=1\ı>j}}^d Q_{ij}(u^{(k)},u^{(l)})\begin{bmatrix} p_{1ijkl} \\ p_{2ijkl} \\ \vdots \\ p_{nijkl} \end{bmatrix}\right), \end{equation} where $Q_0$ and $Q_{ij}$ are the symmetric and anti-symmetric null forms. We present solutions in both the linear and weakly nonlinear regimes. In the linear regime, we show that the coefficients of order $h^3$ determine an injective light-ray transform of a vector field which depends on the coefficients $q_{rkl}, p_{rijkl}$. In the weakly nonlinear regime, we see that the coefficients of order $h$ determine the non-abelian light ray transform for matrices associated with the coefficients. While we do not have an injectivity result for this case, we do have one if we assume the coefficients do not depend on the time variable $x_0$, as our coefficients instead determine an injective non-abelian X-ray transform.

</details>


### [4] [A Pólya--Szegő Theorem for Tangential Polygons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28768)
*Changfeng Gui,Yeyao Hu,Qinfeng Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于任意整数N≥3，在所有给定面积的切触N边形中，正N边形唯一地最大化扭转刚度（torsional rigidity）。证明方法将切触多边形分解为混合Dirichlet-Neumann直角三角单元，其解析核心是某个函数的严格凹性。此外还得到了明确的亏量分解、正多边形扭转刚度随边数严格增加的解析证明，以及切触多边形第一Dirichlet特征值大于等面积正多边形的显式判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Pólya-Szegő定理只处理三角形情形，自然的问题是：在更一般的切触多边形（所有边都与内切圆相切的多边形）中，正多边形是否同样唯一地最大化扭转刚度？本文旨在建立这一更一般情形的定理，并希望提供不依赖于已知结果的独立证明路径，同时为相关特征值比较问题提供可操作的判定准则。

Method: 证明的核心策略是将任意切触N边形分解为N个混合Dirichlet-Neumann边界条件的直角三角单元（每个三角形有一条Dirichlet边和两条Neumann边）。解析核心在于证明函数 α↦h(tanα)−(1/8)tanα 在 α∈(0,π/2) 上严格凹，其中h为混合扭转刚度。证明同时给出了亏量分解：将极值缺失量分离为角度不对称项与周长盈余项两部分。基于此框架，还推导出等面积正多边形扭转刚度随边数严格单调递增的解析证明，以及判定切触多边形第一Dirichlet特征值超过等面积正多边形的显式条件。

Result: 1) 对任意N≥3，正N边形在给定面积的切触N边形中唯一最大化扭转刚度；2) N=3情形自动给出三角形Pólya-Szegő定理的独立证明（因为所有三角形都是切触的）；3) 获得了将角度不对称与周长盈余分离的显式亏量分解；4) 给出了等面积正多边形扭转刚度随边数严格递增的新解析证明；5) 推导出切触多边形第一Dirichlet特征值大于等面积正多边形的显式判据。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典的三角形等周型扭转刚度极值定理推广到所有切触多边形，并提供了不同于经典方法的独立证明。所建立的严格凹性核心引理以及亏量分解公式为后续研究提供了有力工具，也为特征值问题（如等周型Dirichlet特征值不等式）在切触多边形框架下给出了新的判别准则。

Abstract: We prove that, for every integer $N\ge3$, the regular $N$-gon uniquely maximizes torsional rigidity among all tangential $N$-gons of prescribed area. Since every triangle is tangential, the case $N=3$ yields an independent proof of the classical triangular Pólya--Szegő theorem. The proof decomposes a tangential polygon into mixed Dirichlet--Neumann right-triangular cells. Its analytic core is the strict concavity of
  \[
  α\longmapsto h(\tanα)-\frac18\tanα,
  \]
  where $h$ is the mixed torsional rigidity of a right-triangular cell with one Dirichlet side and two Neumann sides, and $α\in(0,π/2)$ is the angle between the Neumann sides. We also obtain an explicit deficit decomposition that separates angular asymmetry from perimeter excess. As applications, we give a novel analytic proof that the torsional rigidity of equal-area regular polygons increases strictly with the number of sides, and derive an explicit criterion ensuring that a tangential polygon has larger first Dirichlet eigenvalue than the equal-area regular polygon.

</details>


### [5] [Nonconcentration of eigenfunctions in Microlocal Kakeya-Nikodym norms: a phase space approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28882)
*Matthew D. Blair*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了在紧致黎曼流形上，特征函数的L^p范数的改进界。作者推广了先前与Sogge合作的工作，将对微局域Kakeya-Nikodym平均改进的充分条件从非正截面曲率推广到更一般的测地流动力学条件（如部分双曲流）。通过高斯波包变换和高斯波束近似，证明了在测地流至少在某些方向上扩张和收缩切向量的情形下，这些平均可以获得增益，从而改进特征函数的L^p界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前作者与Sogge的工作表明，微局域Kakeya-Nikodym平均对改进高频极限下拉普拉斯算子的（近似）特征函数的L^p界具有重要意义，但非正曲率这一几何条件过于严格。本文的动机是找到比非正曲率更一般的充分条件（基于测地流动力学，如部分双曲性），在这些条件下仍能获得Kakeya-Nikodym平均的对数增益，从而在更广泛的几何背景下改进特征函数L^p理论。

Method: 本文采用的方法是：1) 将改进Kakeya-Nikodym平均的充分条件建立在切丛上测地流的动力学性质上，考察流在至少某些方向上扩张和收缩切向量（如部分双曲流）；2) 利用流形上的高斯波包（相空间）变换，以充分挖掘这些动力学假设对微局域平均带来的增益；3) 发展了坐标不变的（coordinate invariant）高斯波束近似来逼近波动方程。

Result: 文章建立了比非正曲率更一般的充分条件，使得Kakeya-Nikodym平均在部分双曲等动力学假设下能够获得（对数）增益，从而在更广泛的黎曼流形上改进特征函数的L^p界，超越了Sogge的普适界。

Conclusion: 本文通过将几何条件（非正曲率）推广为更一般的测地流动力学条件（部分双曲性等），并借助高斯波包变换和坐标不变的高斯波束近似，为改进特征函数的L^p理论提供了更为普适的框架，扩展了先前与Sogge合作工作的适用范围。

Abstract: Previous works of the author and Sogge [BS17], [BS18] showed the significance of microlocal Kakeya-Nikodym averages in improving $L^p$ bounds on (approximate) eigenfunctions of the Laplacian in the high frequency limit. These averages are formed by taking the $L^2$ norm of an eigenfunction when localized in phase space to a small, frequency-dependent tube about a geodesic segment via a pseudodifferential operator. The former work showed that for values of $p$ beneath the Stein-Tomas exponent, $L^p$ norms are controlled by a supremum over these averages. The latter work then showed that when $(M,g)$ has nonpositive sectional curvatures, there is a logarithmic gain in the averages. In combination, these two works improved the $L^p$ theory for eigenfunctions over the universal bounds of Sogge in this geometric setting.
  In the present work, we develop sufficient conditions for improving these averages which are more general than nonpositive curvature. Instead our sufficient conditions are rooted in the dynamics of the geodesic flow on the tangent bundle, considering cases where the flow expands and contracts tangent vectors in at least some directions, e.g. partially hyperbolic flows. We make use of Gaussian wave packet (phase space) transforms on the manifold in order to fully appreciate the gain these hypotheses impart on the microlocal averages. In the process, we further develop Gaussian beam approximations to the wave equation in a coordinate invariant manner.

</details>


### [6] [Perturbed Dyadic Cubes and Quantitative Estimates for Schrödinger Operators with Potentials in $RH^{n/2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28910)
*Jun Cao,Cheng Chen,Chaohong Deng,Yulian Wu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文为带非负势的薛定谔算子构造了一组反映势函数扰动几何的拟立方系统，并利用其定量几何信息刻画了Riesz位势在L^p空间的算子范数，进而给出谱估计应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薛定谔算子L=-Δ+V的谱结构与Riesz位势L^{-α/2}的L^p有界性密切相关，但势函数V的引入会扰动欧氏空间的几何结构，使得经典dyadic立方体方法不再适用。需要构造适应V的几何框架来精确刻画算子范数。

Method: 1) 在势函数V属于逆向Hölder类RH^{n/2}且满足非快速衰减与非剧烈振荡的温和假设下，构造新的dyadic立方体系D^V以反映V扰动的内在几何；2) 利用D^V的定量几何信息（如立方体的尺寸、覆盖性质等），对Riesz位势L^{-α/2}的L^p算子范数进行完整特征化，覆盖所有α∈(0,2]和p∈(1,∞)。

Result: 对所有α∈(0,2]和p∈(1,∞)，完整刻画了Riesz位势L^{-α/2}的L^p算子范数；作为应用，给出了L的若干定量谱估计。

Conclusion: 通过构造与势函数几何相容的dyadic立方体系，本文建立了薛定谔算子Riesz位势算子范数的精确刻画，为后续的谱理论定量分析提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Let $L:=-Δ+V$ be a Schrödinger operator on the Euclidean space $\mathbb{R}^n$ with potential $V$ in the reverse Hölder class $RH^{n/2}$ satisfying some mild assumptions that $V$ neither decays too rapidly nor oscillates violently at infinity. In this paper, the authors construct a new system of dyadic cubes $\mathcal{D}^V$ that reflects the intrinsic geometry perturbed by $V$. Then using the quantitative geometric information of $\mathcal{D}^V$, the authors characterize the $L^p$ operator norm of the Riesz potential $L^{-α/2}$ for all $α\in (0,2]$ and $p\in (1,\infty)$. As applications, some quantitative spectral estimates for $L$ are given.

</details>


### [7] [Local Well-Posedness for a Diffuse Interface Model for Two-Phase Flows from Mixture Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29298)
*Helmut Abels,Harald Garcke,Julia Wittmann*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对ten Eikelder等人提出的描述不可压缩两相流的新扩散界面模型，建立了局部时间适定性结果，这是该模型的首次解析研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的基于单一平均速度的扩散界面模型存在局限性，而新提出的基于混合物理论的模型（为每个相分配各自的动量和质量平衡）尚未有解析研究，需要证明其适定性。

Method: 采用不动点策略进行证明，主要困难在于线性化系统主部的分析。

Result: 成功建立了该两相流扩散界面模型的局部时间适定性（Lipschitz意义下的适定性，包括存在性、唯一性和对初值的连续依赖）。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了该两相流模型在数学解析方面的空白，为后续该模型的深入研究（如全局适定性、渐近行为等）奠定了基础。

Abstract: Local-in-time well-posedness is established for a recently proposed diffuse interface model describing incompressible two-phase flows. The result constitutes the first analytical study of a model introduced by ten Eikelder et al. for the motion of a binary mixture of macroscopically immiscible, viscous, incompressible fluids with unmatched densities. In contrast to classic diffuse interface models based on a single mean velocity, this model is derived within the framework of mixture theory, assigning each phase its own momentum and mass balance, which results in a system of two coupled Navier--Stokes equations and two mass transport equations. The proof of the well-posedness result uses a fixed-point strategy, where the main difficulty lies in the analysis of the principal part of the associated linearized system.

</details>


### [8] [Top-Degree Global Solvability for Tube Complexes in Gevrey Ultradistributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28923)
*Sandro Coriasco,Alexandre Kirilov,Wagner Augusto Almeida de Moraes,Pedro Meyer Tokoro*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究定义在连通非紧实解析流形 M 上的多个 Gevrey 阶闭 1-形式族，证明其在 M×T^m 上对应的微分复形顶阶算子关于 Roumieu Gevrey 超分布全局可解（等价于顶阶上同调消失），无需全局亚椭圆假设，也无需对形式的周期施加算术条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于对非紧流形上多闭 1-形式诱导的微分复形的可解性研究。在紧致背景下，兼容性条件不可避免，且可解性可能依赖于指数小分母条件。本文希望在非紧情形下突破这些限制，证明顶阶算子无条件全局可解，以凸显与非紧/紧设置之间的本质差异。

The associated linear evolution may become singular at its terminal time, and both its maximal lifespan and terminal growth depend on the spatial decay of \(u_0\).

For critical-tail data, an analogous dichotomy holds with the square-root weighted Osgood condition, and the dividing power becomes \(3/2\). Abstract: We develop a two-dimensional Allen-Cahn theory for interfaces with boundary in the line-bundle framework introduced by Fröhlich and Struwe. Near the boundary of an interface, this solution plays the role of the heteroclinic profile in the classical interior theory. However, unlike the interior setting, where the analysis in directions normal to the interface reduces to the ODE satisfied by the heteroclinic, the boundary model has non-flat level sets and must be studied as a genuinely two-dimensional elliptic solution. In the first part of this paper, we construct this model solution and develop its stability and invertibility theory.

The construction uses a Lyapunov-Schmidt reduction, but the boundary introduces a new difficulty: it creates large error terms along the interior of the interface. Controlling these terms requires a refined ansatz and new gluing arguments beyond the standard interior Allen-Cahn theory.

Abstract: We study nonlinear reactive transport in a two-dimensional bulk domain containing a thin branched fracture composed of three narrow branches of thickness epsilon connected through a junction node of diameter O(epsilon). The microscopic model couples nonlinear parabolic reaction-diffusion equations in the bulk with an advection-diffusion equation in the fracture, where the longitudinal Peclet number is of order epsilon^{-1}, leading to advection-dominated transport along the branches. Nonlinear side-dependent flux conditions describe the coupling between the bulk and the fracture. As epsilon tends to zero, the fracture collapses to a one-dimensional graph, and we derive a recurrent structure of effective limit problems: a first-order hyperbolic problem on the graph satisfying the classical Kirchhoff transmission condition at the node, and reaction-diffusion equations in the bulk with nonlinear Robin conditions on the graph edges involving the graph solution. The node boundary conditions of the microscopic model do not affect these leading-order limits. To capture the influence of the node geometry, we construct node-layer and corner-layer correctors and determine subsequent terms of the asymptotic expansion. Their coefficients solve auxiliary boundary-value problems in unbounded domains with outlets at infinity and in corner-type geometries. We assemble a complete multiscale approximation combining bulk, branch, node-layer, and corner-layer contributions, and establish quantitative error estimates in appropriate energy norms. These estimates demonstrate the accuracy of the approximation relative to epsilon and depend explicitly on the corner angles of the limiting graph, reflecting the geometric complexity of the fracture network.

Method: 本文提出"马尔萨斯振荡器"模型，其中振荡器的出生与死亡速率由其相位决定，从而在不改变相位速度的情况下产生人口统计耦合。作者从理论上分析了这种耦合下的群体动力学，证明了同步的可能性、集体频率的选择机制，以及相干性呈现四次方根（fourth-root）的非典型临界行为。关键贡献是推导出精确的降维方案：对具有m个频率类的群体，动力学方程可从高维约化为3m个常微分方程，构成了Ott-Antonsen降维方法在马尔萨斯人口动力学中的对应物。

Result: 研究表明：（1）纯人口统计耦合足以引发群体同步；（2）集体频率会被系统性地选择出来；（3）相干性转变遵循四次方根规律，这与Kuramoto模型的常规平方根临界指数不同，属于非典型（nongeneric）行为；（4）建立了精确的降维框架，m个频率类的系统可简化为3m个常微分方程，为深入的理论分析和数值模拟提供了有效工具。

Conclusion: 马尔萨斯振荡器代表了一类全新的同步机制，其中耦合完全来自人口统计过程（出生和死亡）。这种机制不仅能产生同步和集体频率选择，还呈现出独特的四次方根相变行为，并且具有与Kuramoto系统平行的精确降维结构。该工作拓展了同步理论的边界，为理解不依赖直接相位相互作用的群体节律现象提供了理论框架。

Abstract: A population of oscillators typically synchronizes because coupling pulls their phases together. Here we consider malthusian oscillators whose coupling is demographic: oscillators are born and die at rates determined by their phases, generating an effective coupling without any phase velocity interaction. We find this coupling can synchronize a population, select a collective frequency, and produce a nongeneric fourth-root onset of coherence. These collective dynamics admit an exact reduction: a population with $m$ frequency classes reduces to $3m$ ordinary differential equations. This is the malthusian analogue of the Ott--Antonsen reduction for Kuramoto oscillators.

Abstract: The Retribution and Reciprocity Model (RRM) is a novel framework presented in evolutionary criminology to understand causes of crime through the lens of cooperation. We formalise RRM as an agent-based dynamical system in which criminal propensity evolves through pairwise interactions and through observations of others' interactions. The mathematical model extends bounded confidence opinion dynamics with two novel features: an agent-specific attractor representing the perception of the environment, and the participation of third-party witnesses. We prove that individual propensities remain bounded and establish sufficient conditions for convergence to an extreme value and to the agent's perception of the environment. Simulations show that the dominant system behaviour is not convergence but persistent oscillations, a rare phenomenon in bounded confidence models arising here without the adaptive confidence bounds through which it has previously been obtained. We derive a necessary condition for oscillations, and find that a population sharing a uniform but neutral perception of the environment tends to polarise to the extremes, while a uniform non-neutral perception tends to yield consensus. The polarising regime is confined to a narrow band of near-neutral perceptions, whose width decreases with population size and increases with the strength of reciprocal and retributive tendencies.

  s_{\mathrm{stab}} = \frac{λ_α- 2}{λ_α- 1} + O(α), \] which is evaluated from $α$ and $λ$ at each cut cell at mesh generation time with no time-stepping and no global matrix assembly. For merge-left state redistribution, $|\cos_α(D,v_u)| = 1 - O(α)$, proving SRD acts in the unstable eigendirection. We also characterize which linearized explicit finite-volume operators on scalar periodic 1D cut-cell meshes admit this structure, covering schemes from first-order Godunov to high-order MUSCL with bounded flux coefficients, and prove $\|P_{\cU_α} - P_\cC\| = O(α)$ for $m$ non-adjacent cut cells.

Numerical experiments illustrate the accuracy of the methods and the practical benefit of the polynomial initialization.

Abstract: We introduce an interface multicontinuum homogenization method (I-MCHM) for high-contrast elliptic problems whose subdomains may have distinct microscopic patterns and unequal numbers of continua. Standard subdomain-wise multicontinuum homogenization therefore determines the bulk equations but leaves the interaction among these coarse variables unspecified. Abstract: In multifidelity Monte Carlo (MFMC), optimal sample allocations are typically derived from a continuous relaxation of a variance minimization problem, with integer solutions obtained through post hoc rounding. Such rounding procedures may fail to fully exploit the available cost budget, particularly under tight cost budgets or when model costs vary significantly. In this work, we reformulate the MFMC allocation problem as a variance-constrained cost minimization problem that is equivalent to the standard budget-constrained formulation at the continuous level. This reformulation admits a recursive structure that enables the construction of an integer allocation strategy based on Bellman's principle of optimality. Since the resulting MFMC allocation has a mathematical structure similar to the optimal multilevel Monte Carlo (MLMC) allocation, the proposed strategy naturally extends to MLMC. The resulting algorithm constructs integer-valued sample allocations that more closely follow the continuous variance--cost tradeoff while using the prescribed variance tolerance more efficiently. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach satisfies the prescribed variance tolerance with less computational overhead than standard rounding strategies.

Abstract: Resonant tunneling diodes (RTDs) embedded in an electrical circuit are known for their neuron-like response characteristics, which makes them promising candidates for neuromorphic applications. We further propose a scheme for controlled switching between coexisting stable states which allows to realize tunable memory elements in these circuits. The coupled RTD system exhibits a rich bifurcation structure, showing different degrees of multistability between symmetric and antisymmetric solutions. Limit-cycle branches and their dependence on the system parameters are analyzed using numerical continuation methods. A central focus is placed on the role of symmetry. For two identical RTDs, the system possesses a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ exchange symmetry, which governs the emergence of symmetry-breaking bifurcations and multistable states. The analysis is further generalized to $N$ coupled RTDs, revealing the underlying $S_N$ symmetry structure and its influence on the organization of equilibrium branches, paving the way for neuromorphic network operation.

Abstract: We develop new characterizations of both global and local error bounds for general functions, using directional derivatives and tangent cones without imposing convexity or linear structure. We first establish several equivalent conditions for the global error bound of a nonnegative lower semicontinuous function. These equivalences hold for general, possibly nonconvex and nonsmooth functions. We further link the error bound with perturbation stability, Hausdorff stability of sublevel sets, and an inverse-sublevel-set estimate. Turning to directional derivatives, we introduce the minimal unit-sphere directional derivative \(\varphi(x)\) on the tangent cone and clarify its exact relation with the global slope. For Lipschitz continuous functions we prove that \(\sup_{x\notin S_0} \varphi(x) < 0\) is sufficient for an error bound, and for convex functions on convex sets this condition is also necessary, In finite dimensions we obtain sharp local results: if \(\varphi(\bar{x}) > 0\) at a solution \(\bar{x}\), then a local error bound holds and the optimal constant is exactly \(1/\varphi(\bar{x})\); if \(\varphi(\bar{x}) = 0\) and a suitable direction exists outside the tangent cone of the solution set, the local error bound fails. A general estimate relating the directional derivative to the distance from the tangent cone of the solution set is also derived.

</details>

Abstract: This paper is concerned with the non-Markovian stochastic optimal control problems in which the value function is a random field characterized by a stochastic Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (SHJB) equation. Abstract: We formulate a baseball plate appearance as a finite two-person, zero-sum, extensive-form game with chance moves and imperfect information. Successive pitch cycles are connected through the evolving count, while the Batter chooses an action without observing the Pitcher's current pitch selection. Terminal outcomes are valued through a run-expectancy utility that incorporates both runs scored during the plate appearance and the continuation value of the half-inning. We implement the sequence-form representation of the game, whose linear-programming formulation grows linearly with the game tree and permits exact minimax equilibrium computations for trees far beyond the practical range of the normal form. Nature's transition probabilities are estimated from MLB Statcast data. We also establish two complementary dynamic-programming interpretations. For general finite two-person zero-sum games with perfect recall, we show that the dual variables of the sequence-form best-response programs decompose into reach weights and conditional continuation values. Under the additional state-Markov assumptions of the baseball model, we prove that the full minimax equilibrium can be computed by backward induction over the twelve non-terminal counts. The resulting computations produce equilibrium strategies, continuation values, and conditional and reach-weighted measures of the strategic cost of non-optimal actions.

  term to supply missing directions.

Abstract: We propose GLORO, a bilevel solution methodology for robust optimization involving expensive function evaluations. The methodology is carefully designed to achieve satisfactory optimization results in a computationally efficient manner. It is based on Gaussian Process surrogates in the parameter space for approximate computations of the objective / constraints of the robust optimization problem via Bayesian optimization (lower level), and local models constructed in the variable space using these approximate evaluations (upper level). Both the use of Bayesian optimization (in parameter space) and the use of local models (in variable space) are motivated by the idea of guiding the expensive function evaluations to the regions of interest for the optimization process. The methodological work is motivated by and tested on a robust airfoil design optimization problem. Abstract: We consider the problem of computing the Lebesgue volume of the unit sublevel set of a positive quasi-homogeneous polynomial. The second extracts the volume bounds from a single generalized eigenvalue problem involving moment and localizing matrices whose size grows linearly with the relaxation order, and converges at an exponential rate; the ratio governing this rate is determined by an a priori upper bound on the polynomial over the bounding box. The algebraic and geometric rates therefore transfer to the hierarchy itself, improving on its best known convergence rates.

Abstract: We present an algorithm that finds an epsilon-approximate solution to a mixed integer quadratic programming (MIQP) problem, and that runs on a Turing machine in time polynomial in the size of the instance and in 1/epsilon, provided that the number of integer variables and the number of negative eigenvalues of the Hessian of the objective function are fixed. The main ingredient is a polynomial-time simultaneous diagonalization algorithm: it computes a rational change of basis that maps a given ellipsoid, presented in factored form, exactly to a ball, while making the objective function separable up to an arbitrarily small perturbation and preserving the inertia of its Hessian. The classical construction, in which the objective function is made exactly separable, requires a change of basis that is in general irrational, and cannot be carried out on a Turing machine; ours rests instead on rational Jacobi rotations, which we believe to be of independent interest.

Abstract: We propose and analyze a stochastic direct-search method for unconstrained zeroth-order minimization of locally Lipschitz, possibly nonsmooth, objectives. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first convergence and expected-complexity analysis for an extrapolation-based direct-search method in a nonsmooth stochastic setting. Numerical experiments on a DFO benchmark suite highlight competitive performance against well-established stochastic direct-search methods.

Abstract: Admission control is a widely used mechanism for regulating congestion in stochastic service systems, where restricting arrivals is expected to reduce workload and improve performance.

Abstract: Promoting cooperative behaviour amongst decision makers has key implications for the long term sustainability of social systems. Optimality is here intended in the sense of cost minimisation. Here, we provide and optimal incentive strategy for structured populations in general network, a rather unexplored case. Further, we look at populations where, through the network structure, each player interact with one set of neighbours while contributing to an opinion diffusion dynamics from a second set of neighbours. Hence we cover the case of multilayer networks. To fill this gap, we provide a solution to the optimal control problem by solving the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation and derive an analytic solution for distributing incentives in multilayer networks. By implementing the dynamics of the networked Prisoner's Dilemma Game, we provide a feedback control loop that yields an optimal incentive distribution over time. The dynamic incentive depends on the current state of cooperation, the game-layer network, the strategy-diffusion layer, and the payoff matrix design. We found that the optimal incentive is node-wise, unique to each node on the network, influenced by its relative position according to the adjacency matrices of both layers. We also found that the optimal solution is not exclusively reward or punishment. While one node can receive a reward, the other node may receive punishment at the same time, depending on its current level of cooperation, relative to other nodes. We provide analytic solutions and numerical validations for the cases studied, comparing our results to the existing literature.

Abstract: For a given real matrix $A\in\R^{n\times n}$, a Pareto eigenvalue of $A$ is a real number $λ\in\R$ for which there exists a nonzero vector $x\in\R^n\setminus\{0\}$ such that $$ 0\leq x\perp Ax-λx\geq0. $$ We prove that every matrix $A\in\R^{4\times4}$ has at most $23$ distinct Pareto eigenvalues. We first prove the result for matrices of order $4$ satisfying two conditions: every real eigenvalue of a principal submatrix is simple, and two different principal submatrices have no real eigenvalue in common. Thus only $25$ remains to exclude. We exclude this case by using the support profiles in order $3$ and identities involving eigenvectors of principal submatrices. A perturbation and fixed point index argument then extends the bound to all real matrices of order $4$. </details>

Abstract: This paper presents a method for computing inner polytopic approximations of admissible sets for continuous-time linear control systems subject to affine state constraints. The boundary samples are converted into a half-space representation, and runtime complexity analyses are derived for both the sampling and polytope reconstruction algorithms. An extension of the framework to Hammerstein systems is also presented.

Abstract: This paper presents a Matlab implementation of an arc-search infeasible interior point algorithm for linear programming (LP), which has a proven polynomial bound of $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{n}L)$, the best among all interior-point algorithms for LP. Software architecture and major functions are discussed. Its ease of use is described by a simple example. Crucial strategies are summarized. Quality of the software is assured because this software has been extensively tested on both PC and Linux for the widely used Netlib benchmark linear programming problems in standard form. Some benchmark test problems involve tens of thousands of constraints and hundreds of thousands of variables. For all tested problems, the code found the optimal solution. The numerical results have been compared to those obtained by the popular Mehrotra's predictor-corrector algorithm. We conclude that the implemented algorithm not only has the best polynomial bound but also is computationally competitive compared to the popular Mehrotra's predictor-corrector algorithm.

### [63] [Sign compression for Muon: SignMuon, MuonSign, and the Limits of Error Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29674)
*Maria Smirnova,Alexey Kravatskiy*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析SignMuon优化器——将Muon更新按元素符号压缩为每参数1比特。研究发现：无论将符号操作放在LMO之前还是两侧，算法在构造的显式实例上都会上升；对Muon输出做误差反馈（ER）无法修复问题，但对梯度做误差反馈（EF21-MuonUSign和EF21-MuonSign）可以收敛到标准O(T^{-1/2})速率。然而实验（集中式CIFAR-10、联邦CIFAR-10、nanoGPT speedrun）显示，被证明发散的"符号在LMO之后"的启发式方案实际表现最强，优于有理论保证的收敛变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极低通信预算下运行矩阵感知优化器（Muon）的需求日益增长，而最直接的压缩方式是对Muon更新取元素符号（SignMuon）。虽然SignMuon在实践中优于SignSGD，但缺乏理论保证。作者旨在探究：符号放置的位置（LMO前后或两侧）是否能保证下降、误差反馈能否修复压缩误差、以及理论保证与现实性能之间的关系。

Method: （1）构建显式小实例证明sign-before (MuonUSign) 和 sign-on-both-sides (MuonSign) 在线性函数上也会上升，证明无任何符号放置方式能保证下降；（2）理论上分析误差反馈的应用位置：对Muon输出应用误差反馈可完全失效（对任意光滑常数、步长和动量），而对梯度应用误差反馈（EF21框架下）可获得标准收敛速率；（3）在集中式CIFAR-10、联邦式CIFAR-10和nanoGPT speedrun上进行实验对比。

Result: 理论上证明SignMuon的所有符号放置变体（前、后、两侧）均可能发散；对输出做误差反馈无效，但对梯度做误差反馈可达到EF21标准O(T^{-1/2})收敛率。实证上，所有基准（集中式CIFAR-10、联邦CIFAR-10、nanoGPT speedrun）中，符号放在LMO之后的启发式方案（被证明发散）始终是最强压缩方法，而理论上保证收敛的变体反而表现较差。

Conclusion: 在低比特压缩的矩阵感知优化器中，理论收敛保证与大规模实际性能之间存在显著矛盾：被证明发散的启发式方法（sign-after-LMO）在实践中表现最佳。这表明在该尺度下，压缩策略的启发式设计比理论保证更为重要，而误差反馈只有在正确的应用位置（对梯度而非对优化器输出）才能发挥修复作用。

Abstract: SignMuon compresses the Muon update to one bit per parameter by taking its elementwise sign, providing the most direct way to run a matrix-aware optimizer under an extremely low communication budget. It outperforms SignSGD in practice, yet it can ascend even on a linear function. Signing the gradient before the Linear Minimization Oracle (LMO), rather than after, does not repair this: we construct a small explicit instance on which sign-before (MuonUSign) and sign-on-both-sides (MuonSign) ascend as well, so no placement of the sign around the oracle descends in general. Error feedback, the standard remedy for a biased compressor, does not rescue SignMuon: when applied to Muon's output, error feedback can fail for every smoothness constant, step size, and momentum. Applied to the gradient, error feedback does work, and EF21-MuonUSign and EF21-MuonSign attain the standard $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$ rate for the squared gradient norm on smooth nonconvex problems, the latter at one bit in each direction. Experiments then reverse the ordering: across centralized CIFAR-10, federated CIFAR-10, and the nanoGPT speedrun, the strongest compressed method is consistently sign-after-the-LMO, precisely the placement we prove divergent, with the provably convergent variants trailing it. Compressing after the LMO, a heuristic, matters more at these scales than the guarantee does.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机扫描Gibbs采样器是高斯图模型的重要推断工具，但其收敛速率通常受图拓扑强烈影响，在大型图上收敛缓慢。作者希望通过Fourier变换将对偶域中运用，从而获得拓扑无关的加速收敛，并建立两个域间统计量的对应关系。

Method: 利用Fourier变换对正常因子图局部因子进行变换得到对偶模型，在原始域和对偶域中分别推导齐次k-正则图的精确收敛速率；通过图论工具（代数连通度）刻画对偶域的有效收敛速率；推导原域与对偶域协方差结构间的显式代数关系。

Result: 证明了对所有含环的同质图模型，对偶域Gibbs采样收敛速率是普适的且独立于图拓扑；有效收敛速率由图的代数连通度决定，且不增加每次扫描的计算复杂度；建立了原域与对偶域协方差结构的显式代数映射，实现对偶域统计量直接恢复原域边缘统计量；数值实验验证了多类图族上的理论收敛速率改进。

Conclusion: 对偶域变换是一种有效的加速策略，在不增加每次扫描计算开销的前提下显著提升Gibbs采样收敛速率，并通过解析关系实现对偶域推断结果到原模型的直接映射，具有理论与计算双重优势。

Abstract: We study the convergence properties of the random-sweep Gibbs sampler for Gaussian graphical models with a thin-membrane prior. We demonstrate that the convergence rate of the Gibbs sampler is significantly accelerated in the dual model, which is obtained by applying the Fourier transform to the local factors of the normal factor graph representing the original model. In both domains, we derive the exact convergence rates for homogeneous $k$-regular graphs. We prove that, for all homogeneous models whose graphical representations contain cycles, the convergence rate in the dual domain is universal and independent of the underlying graph topology. Moreover, we show that the effective convergence rate in the dual domain is governed by the algebraic connectivity of the graph, providing an additional acceleration without increasing the computational complexity per sweep. We further establish an explicit algebraic relation between the covariance structures of the primal and dual models, enabling marginal statistics of the primal model to be recovered directly from those of the dual model. Finally, numerical experiments on several graph families confirm our theoretical results and demonstrate substantial improvements in the convergence rates in various settings.

</details>

*Silas Koemen*

Main category: stat.ML

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于树的扩散模型在拟合表格回归条件分布时，直接继承了神经扩散模型的默认设计选择（如加噪路径、参数化、训练分布、特征和采样器），但这些默认并非为树模型优化，反而成为性能的约束瓶颈。作者希望揭示梯度提升集成实际解决的监督回归问题的条件化机制，并释放这些默认设计的束缚，以提升表格回归中条件密度估计的性能与效率。

Method: DiffGBM采用两个技术轴：（1）高斯路径流匹配训练器，直接学习p(y|x)的速度场，通过代数关系恢复得分，支持少步确定性ODE采样；（2）score-flex空间，将得分侧的多个配方组件（残差化、EDM风格预条件、log-sigma时间采样、噪声水平特征、损失加权、直方图分辨率）视为在共享LightGBM表面上的联合可调轴，而非冻结的捆绑设计，使已发布的配方成为score-flex空间中的特例。

Result: 在11个表格基准上使用fold-0调参、folds-1~5评估，并以匹配的40次试验预算和采样器进行比较：选定的score-flex配置在每个数据集上都击败了基线（配对Wilcoxon 11/0，p<10^-3），最优聚合CRPS技能为0.725对比0.699。score-flex行通过随机采样器获得精度优势但最慢；流匹配是最廉价采样器（比已发布基线快5.2倍）且是最佳校准的DiffGBM行。调优后的非扩散基线仍在个别数据集上胜出，随机（ε>0）流采样器未Pareto支配确定性角点。

Conclusion: 论文表明树基扩散模型的性能瓶颈来自继承的神经扩散默认设计而非模型本身。通过将得分侧配方分解为可调参数空间（score-flex）并引入高斯路径流匹配，DiffGBM在准确率和采样效率之间提供了互补的权衡：score-flex追求精度但代价是计算，流匹配则提供最快的采样和最佳校准。但研究也承认调优的非扩散基线在个别数据集上仍具竞争力，且随机流采样在Pareto意义上没有超越确定性采样。

Abstract: Tree-based diffusion models fit flexible conditional predictive distributions for tabular regression without a neural density estimator, but they inherit their design defaults---noising path, parameterization, training distribution, features, sampler---from the neural setting. We show these defaults are the binding constraint: what a gradient-boosted ensemble actually solves is a supervised regression problem whose conditioning they determine. We present DiffGBM, which makes them explicit along two axes. First, a Gaussian-path flow-matching trainer for $p(y \mid x)$ that learns a velocity field directly and recovers the score algebraically, admitting few-step deterministic ODE sampling. Second, we expose the score-side recipe---residualization, EDM-style preconditioning, log-sigma time sampling, noise-level features, loss weighting, and histogram resolution---as jointly tunable axes over a shared LightGBM surface rather than one frozen bundle. This \emph{score-flex} space represents the published recipe as a special case; across eleven tabular benchmarks under fold-0 tuning, folds-1--5 evaluation, and a matched 40-trial budget and sampler, the selected configurations beat that baseline on \emph{every} dataset (paired Wilcoxon $11/0$, $p<10^{-3}$), with the best aggregate CRPS skill (0.725 vs.\ 0.699) of any row. The two rows are complementary: score-flex buys accuracy with a stochastic sampler and is the slowest row, while flow matching is the cheapest sampler ($5.2\times$ faster than the published baseline) and the best-calibrated DiffGBM row. Tuned non-diffusion baselines still win individual datasets, and stochastic ($\varepsilon>0$) flow samplers do not Pareto-dominate the deterministic corner.

Abstract: Variance-based global sensitivity analysis (GSA) plays a key role in uncertainty quantification by identifying the contributions of uncertain inputs to the variability of the model response. The repeated model evaluations required for these tasks are often prohibitively expensive; surrogate models provide an efficient alternative by constructing inexpensive approximations of the underlying system response. Constructing surrogate models that combine scalability and interpretability for systems with high-dimensional stochastic inputs and functional responses remains challenging, particularly when sensitivity estimates are required across spatial or temporal domains. Polynomial chaos expansion (PCE) provides an effective framework for uncertainty propagation and sensitivity analysis due to its orthogonal structure and direct relationship with variance-based sensitivity measures. However, PCE suffers from the curse of dimensionality, whose computational burden is amplified for problems with functional responses. In this work, we introduce the Structured Neural Chaos (sNC) expansion as a surrogate modeling framework for variance-based GSA, inspired by the interpretability and orthogonal structure of PCE. The proposed framework retains the interpretability of structured decompositions while leveraging the expressive power of neural networks. The sNC expansion mirrors a truncated functional ANOVA decomposition, where each interaction component admits a separable low-rank approximation whose basis functions and coefficients are parameterized by neural networks. The expansion is constructed sequentially, adaptively identifying the dominant modes within each ANOVA subspace and determining the effective complexity of the representation. The resulting structure enables the extraction of statistical and sensitivity quantities directly from the coefficients of the sNC expansion at negligible cost.

Abstract: Modern opaque AI models prize performance over interpretability, which makes testing difficult. However, formal statistical tests conducted on a model's embedding space can provide robust characterizations of semantic structure, concept separation, and knowledge graph alignment. Model developers would benefit from a model comparison technique that leverages human-curated knowledge structures to test alignment. The scale of the input space for even relatively simple tasks motivates the need for alignment checks that augment standard outcome reasoning. This work develops and demonstrates a topology-based multi-modal alignment test to make deployment, selection, and comparison of opaque models more interpretable. These methods also offer an intuitive connection to possibility theory and a unified decision theoretic framework from data to deployment.

### [68] [Simple-regret rates and minimax optimality of fixed-prior expected improvement in Matérn and squared-exponential RKHSs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29245)
*Emmanuel Vazquez,Sébastien Petit*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了确定性目标函数最小化中的期望改进（EI）策略。假设目标函数属于连续正半定核 k 的 RKHS，函数值精确观测，EI 由固定零均值高斯过程模型计算。作者用特征空间中创新的范数来刻画归一化后验标准差，通过Gram行列式和Kolmogorov宽度估计序列分离半径，结合单步遗憾不等式，获得了有限预算下的简单遗憾界。对Matérn核简单遗憾为O(N^{-ν/d})，对平方指数核为O(exp[-c·min{N, N^{1/d}log(eN)}])（近似EI最大化）或O(exp[-c·N^{1/d}log(eN)])（精确EI最大化）。当可行集有非空内部且B>0时，精确EI策略在Matérn核和平方指数核下均达到极小极大速率最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 期望改进（EI）策略是贝叶斯优化中最常用的采集函数之一，但其理论保证，特别是对简单遗憾的收敛速率分析，此前缺乏严格的结果。作者希望通过建立EI策略在确定性精确观测下的有限预算遗憾界，验证EI策略在极小极大意义下的最优性。

Method: 作者将候选点x的归一化后验标准差与特征空间中对应创新（即k(x,·)正交于先前评估点代表元的成分）的范数等同起来。引入序列分离半径来界定任意查询序列上排名的创新范数，利用Gram行列式和Kolmogorov宽度对不同维数子空间估计这些半径。结合单步遗憾不等式，推导出有限预算下的简单遗憾界。通过近似EI最大化（至少取最大EI的固定正比例）和精确EI最大化两种情形分别给出结果。

Result: 对N次后初始查询，各向同性Matérn核（平滑性ν>0）的简单遗憾为O(N^{-ν/d})；各向同性平方指数核在近似EI最大化下为O(exp[-c_1·min{N, N^{1/d}log(eN)}])，在精确EI最大化下为O(exp[-c_2·N^{1/d}log(eN)])。这些界对RKHS球半径B≥0一致成立。当X有非空内部且B>0时，精确EI策略对Matérn核达到极小极大速率最优，对平方指数核在指数上的常数意义上达到极小极大速率最优。

Conclusion: 该工作首次建立了EI策略在精确观测确定性优化中的有限预算简单遗憾收敛速率，证明了EI策略在Matérn和平方指数核下具有极小极大最优性，为EI在贝叶斯优化中的广泛应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We study the expected improvement (EI) policy for minimizing a deterministic objective function $f$ on a nonempty compact set $\mathcal X \subset\mathbb R^d$. We assume that $f$ belongs to the RKHS $\mathcal H_k$ of a continuous positive-semidefinite kernel $k$ on $\mathcal X$. Function values are observed exactly, and EI is computed from a fixed zero-mean Gaussian-process model with covariance $σ^2k$. After an initial design, the policy queries a point whose EI is at least a fixed positive fraction of its maximum.
  We identify the normalized posterior standard deviation at a candidate point $x$ with the norm of the corresponding innovation in the canonical feature space, namely the component of $k(x,\cdot)$ orthogonal to the span of the preceding evaluation representers. Sequential separation radii bound the ranked innovation norms along arbitrary query sequences. We estimate these radii using Gram determinants and Kolmogorov widths for subspaces of different dimensions, then combine the estimates with a one-step regret inequality to obtain finite-budget bounds for simple regret.
  After $N$ post-initial queries, simple regret is $O(N^{-ν/d})$ for isotropic Matérn kernels of smoothness $ν>0$. For the isotropic squared-exponential kernel, simple regret is $O(\exp[-c_1\min\{N, N^{1/d}\log(eN)\}])$ for some $c_1>0$. With exact EI maximization, it is $O(\exp[-c_2N^{1/d} \log(eN)])$ for some $c_2>0$. For every fixed $B\geq0$, these bounds are uniform over the RKHS ball of radius $B$.
  If $\mathcal X$ has nonempty interior and $B>0$, then, among deterministic methods whose final recommendation may be any point of $\mathcal X$, the exact EI policy is minimax-rate optimal over the RKHS ball of radius $B$ for Matérn kernels and minimax-rate optimal up to constants in the exponent for squared-exponential kernels.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多臂老虎机文献中的算法大多提供渐近后悔保证，但许多实际应用在有限且外部强加的时域内运行，现有算法在有限时间范围下的表现缺乏理论保证，因此需要专门为有限时间范围设计并分析算法。

Method: 提出一类正则化贪心算法（regularized greedy algorithms）用于多臂伯努利老虎机；推导出有限时间后悔上界的解析形式，将后悔分解为瞬时探索成本和随正则化强度指数衰减的次优收敛项；基于该分解推导正则化参数的理论校准规则，并将经典贪心策略作为其极限情形进行分析。

Result: 首次给出了正则化贪心老虎机的有限时间后悔包络；证明了有限时间后悔可分解为瞬时探索成本与指数衰减的收敛项；基于该理论推导出正则化参数的校准规则，并获得了经典贪心策略的更优后悔保证。大量数值实验表明，校准后的正则化贪心策略持续持平或优于最先进的算法。

Conclusion: 正则化贪心策略可以为有限时间范围的老虎机问题提供一种有效的方法，兼具理论保证与实际性能，可以作为实际应用中有吸引力的选择。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly rely on sequential experimentation to improve decision-making. While the multi-armed bandit literature has developed algorithms with strong asymptotic regret guarantees, many practical applications operate over finite and externally imposed horizons. Motivated by the finite-horizon setting, we develop a class of regularized greedy algorithms for multi-armed Bernoulli bandits. We derive the first finite-horizon regret envelopes for regularized greedy bandits, showing that finite-horizon regret decomposes into transient exploration costs and a suboptimal convergence term that decays exponentially with the regularization strength. This characterization yields principled calibration rules for the regularization parameters and, as a limiting case, sharper regret guarantees for the classical greedy policy. Across extensive numerical experiments, calibrated regularized greedy policies consistently match or outperform state-of-the-art algorithms. These results suggest that regularized greedy policies can provide an effective approach for finite-horizon bandit problems.

### [70] [Analytical and Bootstrap Confidence Intervals of Double Machine Learning: Simulation studies and an application to rural-urban difference in obesity prevalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29456)
*Haozheng Xu,Siyuan Ma,Qingyan Xiang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模模拟研究和真实数据分析，系统比较了双机器学习（DML）在不同机器学习算法（OLS、LASSO、随机森林、LightGBM、神经网络）下的置信区间覆盖率，发现学习者选择对DML推断的可靠性有重大影响，且样本量增加时覆盖率反而下降的反直觉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DML方法虽然允许使用灵活的机器学习模型估计nuisance参数并保持有效推断，但实际应用中研究者需要从众多算法中选择，而这一选择对DML方差估计的影响尚未被充分刻画。现有理论没有明确指导实践者如何选择学习器以保证推断可靠性。

Method: 通过全面模拟研究，比较了（1）DML理论推导的分析置信区间与（2）bootstrap置信区间，在多种数据生成设置下使用OLS、LASSO、随机森林、LightGBM和神经网络五种学习器，评估偏差、置信区间宽度和覆盖率等指标，并使用美国县级农村-城市差异的真实数据进行验证。

Result: 结果显示分析置信区间与bootstrap置信区间在覆盖率上存在显著差异，学习者选择对DML推断的可靠性至关重要。反直觉的是，在许多场景下样本量增大时两种置信区间的覆盖率反而下降。真实数据分析发现模型性能随学习者选择而变化，且更高程度的农村化对县级肥胖患病率具有统计显著的递增效应。

Conclusion: 学习者选择是影响DML推断可靠性的关键因素，实务研究者在应用DML时需要谨慎对待置信区间的覆盖率保证，因为无论分析型还是bootstrap型置信区间都可能偏离名义覆盖率，且样本量的增加并不能保证覆盖率的改善。

Abstract: Double Machine Learning (DML) is a popular approach for treatment effect estimation in various settings, which allows a wide range of flexible machine learning methods to be used for nuisance parameter estimation while preserving valid inference. In practice, however, applied researchers must choose among many machine learning algorithms for nuisance models, and the impact of this choice on the variance estimation of DML is not well characterized. We conduct a comprehensive simulation study to compare the coverage probability of DML confidence intervals across different machine learning algorithms. In this study, we compare (1) analytical confidence intervals derived by DML theory versus (2) bootstrap confidence interval. We use a set of learners including ordinary least squares, LASSO, Random Forest, LightGBM, and Neural Networks under different data generation settings. We evaluate the performance across difference settings by bias, confidence interval width, and most importantly, coverage probability. Our results show substantial variability in coverage performance across analytical and bootstrap confidence intervals, highlighting that learner choice plays a critical role in reliable DML inference. Surprisingly, we find that in many settings, when sample size increases, the coverage probability of both DML analytical and bootstrap confidence interval decreases. We further investigate coverage probabilities using a real dataset on rural urban differences among U.S. counties. The real data analysis discovers that (1) the model performance still varies by the learner choices and (2) greater rurality has a statistically significant increasing effect on county level obesity prevalence.

</details>

### [71] [Topology-Aware Data Movement for Disaggregated GPU Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28633)
*Sanjeev Rao Ganjihal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种拓扑感知的KV缓存传输编排器，用于解决大规模LLM推理中预填充（prefill）和解码（decode）分离时KV缓存传输的网络瓶颈问题。系统利用互连拓扑感知、流水线分层传输、NVLink域感知的专家放置以及CXL 3.0内存扩展等机制，在异构互连（NVLink、InfiniBand、TCP）下实现3至18倍的传输延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解聚式LLM推理（Disaggregated LLM inference）中，KV缓存在预填充池和解码池之间的传输成为数据中心网络瓶颈。现有系统（如DistServe、Splitwise、Mooncake）均使用统一RDMA传输，忽略了GPU间带宽因物理位置关系存在高达72倍的差异（NVLink 900GB/s、InfiniBand 50GB/s、TCP 12.5GB/s）。这一忽视导致传输效率低下，在70B模型下每请求需传输2.6GB KV缓存，生产规模下聚合需求超100GB/s，现有方案无法正确处理这一网络问题。

Method: 设计了一个拓扑感知的传输编排器，包含三个核心机制：(1) 流水线化的逐层传输，将传输与进行中的预填充计算重叠，隐藏60%-85%的传输延迟；(2) NVLink域感知的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）模型放置，联合优化专家调度与KV缓存局部性；(3) 利用CXL 3.0内存扩展器作为共享溢出层，相比NVMe提供6倍容量和86倍更低的延迟。系统在启动时自动发现互连层次结构，并为每次传输选择最优传输路径。

Result: 由于需要多节点异构互连集群和CXL 3.0硬件（超出学术资源范围且GPU云中尚不可用），文中提供了分析带宽模型、组件实现及跨三种架构的投影分析，结果显示相比统一RDMA方案，传输延迟降低3至18倍。

Conclusion: 拓扑感知的KV缓存传输编排是对现有均匀RDMA假设的重要改进，通过充分利用异构互连层次结构和计算传输重叠，可显著提升解聚式LLM推理的性能。但全面评估受限于硬件可用性，未来需在真实异构集群上进行验证。

Abstract: Disaggregated LLM inference creates a datacenter networking problem that no existing system solves correctly. When prefill and decode run on separate GPU pools, the KV cache must be transferred between them. For a 70B model this is 2.6 GB per request, exceeding 100 GB/s aggregate at production scale. Yet DistServe, Splitwise, and Mooncake all use uniform RDMA, ignoring that bandwidth between two GPUs varies by 72x depending on their physical relationship: 900 GB/s via NVLink within a domain, 50 GB/s via InfiniBand across nodes, 12.5 GB/s via TCP across data centers. We design a topology-aware transfer orchestrator that discovers interconnect hierarchy at startup and selects optimal transport per transfer. Three mechanisms work together: (1) pipelined layer-by-layer transfer that overlaps transmission with ongoing prefill, hiding 60 to 85 percent of latency behind computation; (2) NVLink domain-aware placement for Mixture-of-Experts models that co-optimizes expert dispatch with KV cache locality; and (3) CXL 3.0 memory expanders as a shared overflow tier providing 6x capacity at 86x lower latency than NVMe. Full evaluation requires multi-node clusters with heterogeneous interconnects and CXL 3.0 hardware that is beyond academic resources and not yet available in GPU clouds. We present analytical bandwidth models, component implementations, and projected analysis across three architectures showing 3 to 18x transfer latency reduction over uniform RDMA.

</details>


### [72] [Frugal Bayesian Optimization: Scalable Surrogates for Data- and Resource-Limited Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29225)
*Panagiotis Krokidas,Christoforos Rekatsinas,Vassilis Sioros,Grigorios M. Chatziathanasiou,Efi-Maria Papia,George Giannakopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算感知的贝叶斯优化（BO）研究框架FruBO，系统评估了四种代理模型在八个基准函数和九个真实数据集上的优化质量与计算开销，发现高斯过程为基础的BO计算成本最高但性能并不占优，并据此引入了一个基于廉价数据集特征预测最优代理模型的推荐框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管贝叶斯优化被广泛用于科学和工程应用中的数据高效优化，但其计算成本很少与优化性能一起被评估。现有研究大多只关注优化质量而忽视计算开销，缺乏一个系统性的计算感知基准来指导代理模型的选择。

Method: 在八个基准函数和九个真实世界数据集（覆盖材料科学、力学、机器人学、化学和机器学习）上，沿两个维度——优化质量和计算节俭性——系统评估四种代理模型：高斯过程、随机森林、NGBoost和贝叶斯自适应样条曲面。基于发现，进一步提出一个代理模型推荐框架，利用低成本的数据集特征预测最合适的BO代理模型。

Result: 基于高斯过程的BO始终产生最高的时间和内存开销，但并未带来更优的优化性能或样本效率；可扩展的替代模型以极小计算成本即可达到相同或更好的性能。推荐框架能有效根据数据集特征预测合适的代理模型。

Conclusion: FruBO为贝叶斯优化提供了可复现的、计算感知的基准，并为在有限计算和实验预算下选择代理模型提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) is widely adopted for data-efficient optimization in scientific and engineering applications, yet its computational cost is rarely evaluated alongside optimization performance. Here we present a systematic, compute-aware study of BO that evaluates surrogate models along two axes: optimization quality and computational frugality. Across eight benchmark functions and nine real-world datasets spanning materials science, mechanics, robotics, chemistry, and machine learning, we benchmark four surrogate models: Gaussian Processes, Random Forests, NGBoost, and Bayesian Adaptive Spline Surfaces. We show that Gaussian Process-based BO consistently incurs the highest time and memory overhead without delivering superior optimization or sample efficiency. In contrast, scalable alternatives achieve equal or better performance at a fraction of the computational cost. Motivated by these findings, we introduce a surrogate-recommendation framework that predicts the most suitable BO surrogate from inexpensive dataset characteristics. Together, these results establish FruBO as a reproducible, compute-aware baseline for Bayesian Optimization and provide practical guidance for surrogate selection under limited computational and experimental budgets.

</details>


### [73] [Sensitivity Analysis of GRU, LSTM and Transformer Encoder in Classification of Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28665)
*Bidhya Shrestha,Christos Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何仅使用车辆远程信息处理数据识别Level 2级别的自动驾驶系统（包括Comma Openpilot、Tesla Autopilot和Cadillac Super Cruise）以及手动驾驶。作者评估了三种序列分类模型（GRU、LSTM和Transformer编码器）在干净数据上的表现，并引入了一个模块化鲁棒性评估框架，通过五类污染（五种严重级别）模拟真实遥测数据退化。结果显示事件级污染影响较小，而时间抖动会导致宏F1分数大幅下降至0.44-0.50。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动驾驶系统和软件定义车辆的普及，独立监控系统以验证哪个自动化驾驶系统处于活动状态变得至关重要，这涉及安全监控、法规合规、保险评估和异常检测。现有研究缺乏对这种监控系统的系统性评估，尤其是在真实数据退化条件下的鲁棒性分析。

Method: 首先评估三种序列分类模型（GRU、LSTM、Transformer编码器）在干净数据上识别三种Level 2 ADS系统（Comma Openpilot、Tesla Autopilot、Cadillac Super Cruise）和手动驾驶的能力，并采用威胁匹配训练策略。其次引入模块化鲁棒性评估框架，通过五类污染族（加性高斯白噪声+累积漂移、相关跨通道噪声、时间抖动、事件信号突发丢失/延迟转换/虚假切换/跨特征不一致）在五个严重级别（L1-L5）下模拟真实遥测退化，使用宏F1作为评估指标。

Result: 干净数据上三种模型均表现良好：GRU宏F1为0.92，LSTM为0.90，Transformer编码器为0.93。威胁匹配训练可达到0.904-0.916的宏F1，且干净数据性能仅有小幅损失。鲁棒性评估显示事件级污染影响较小（L5级别仍不低于0.87），而时间抖动使三种模型的宏F1大幅下降至0.44-0.50。

Conclusion: 序列分类模型能够有效通过遥测数据识别不同的Level 2自动驾驶系统，但时间抖动是主要的鲁棒性弱点，需要在实际监控系统设计中加以重点防护。

Abstract: Automated driving systems (ADSs) are becoming ubiquitous. Future Software Defined Vehicles (SDVs) may be able to run multiple ADSs, both native and aftermarket such as Comma.ai's Openpilot. Monitoring systems to independently verify which automated driving system is active are important for safety monitoring, regulatory compliance, insurance assessment, and anomaly detection. In this paper, we first evaluate the effectiveness of three sequence-based classification models: Gated Recurrent Units (GRU), Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, and a Transformer encoder model for identifying Level 2 automated driving systems using vehicle telematics data alone: Comma Openpilot, Tesla Autopilot, and Cadillac Super Cruise, along with manual driving. All three models achieve strong clean-data performance with macro F1-scores of 0.92 (GRU), 0.90 (LSTM), and 0.93 (Transformer encoder model) when trained on clean data; threat-matched training yields 0.904-0.916 macro F1 with only a modest clean-data penalty. Second, we introduce a modular robustness evaluation framework that simulates realistic telematics degradation through five corruption families at five severity levels (L1-L5). Continuous channels are perturbed using additive white Gaussian noise with cumulative drift, correlated cross-channel noise, and temporal jitter. Binary event signals are subjected to burst loss, delayed transitions, spurious toggles and cross-feature inconsistencies inspired by communication errors. Robustness is measured using macro-F1, which gives equal weight to each class and is suitable for imbalanced multiclass evaluation. Our evaluation reveals a sharp failure-mode split: event-level corruptions reduce macro-F1 only slightly (greater than equal to 0.87 at L5), while temporal jitter collapses macro-F1 to 0.44-0.50 across GRU, LSTM, and Transformer encoder model.

</details>


### [74] [Guarantees on Dynamical System Distinguishability for LLM Token Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28667)
*Mohamed Akrout,Dan Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上分析了基于动力系统（DS）的大语言模型响应分类方法，将其形式化为两个随机线性DS之间的二元假设检验问题，证明了分类错误概率随序列长度指数衰减，并刻画了跨嵌入模型泛化的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的基于动力系统视角的LLM响应分类方法虽然经验上表现良好，但缺乏理论解释。具体需要回答三个问题：为什么这种方法有效？分类精度如何随token序列长度扩展？跨嵌入模型时性能如何迁移？

Method: 将分类任务形式化为两个随机线性动力系统之间的二元假设检验。通过分析两个DS平稳边际分布之间的全变差距离，证明其可以任意小（即使动力学差异显著），从而给出忽略动力学信息的分类器的精度下限。进一步推导出DS分类方法的误分类概率以序列长度L的指数形式衰减，衰减速率由捕获两个DS谱距离的动力学判别量δ²决定。同时引入嵌入模型间的近似交织条件，利用交织映射的最小奇异值建立可迁移判别性的下界。

Result: 证明了（1）忽略token动力学的分类器存在根本的精度下限；（2）DS-based分类方法的误分类概率随序列长度指数衰减，衰减率由δ²控制；（3）跨嵌入模型迁移的判别性下界与交织映射的最小奇异值相关联。

Conclusion: 这些理论结果解释了DS-based分类方法的经验表现，并激励了利用DS理论分析AI系统的进一步研究，与常见的"用AI建模动力系统"的研究方向形成互补。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that classifying large language models (LLMs)' responses can be distinguished by modeling token embeddings as trajectories of a black-box dynamical system (DS) and comparing prediction residuals of two DSs. Despite the empirical success of this dynamical approach, a theoretical understanding of why it works, how well it scales as a function of the token sequence, and when it transfers across embedding models remains lacking. We address these questions by formalizing the classification task as a binary hypothesis test between two stochastic linear DSs. We show that the total variation distance between the stationary marginal distributions of the two DSs can be arbitrarily small even when the dynamics differ substantially, which provides a fundamental accuracy floor for any classifier that ignores token dynamics. We then show that the misclassification probability of DS-based classification decays exponentially in the sequence length $L$, with the decay governed by a dynamical discriminability quantity $δ^2$ that captures the spectral distance between the two DSs. We also characterize cross-embedding generalization by introducing an approximate intertwining condition between embedding models and establishing a lower bound on the transferable discriminability in terms of the intertwining map's smallest singular value. Together, these results explain the empirical performance of DS-based classification and motivate further investigation into using DS theory to analyze AI systems, in contrast to the more common approach of using AI to model dynamical systems.

</details>


### [75] [LARA: Lightweight Adapters in the Residual Stream for Composable Adaptation and Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28669)
*Pascal Ekin,Hyosun Choi,Wei Jie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LARA是一种轻量级加性残差适应方法，在冻结模型的残差流中而非权重中操作。与LoRA在权重矩阵上添加低秩更新不同，LARA在少量层读取隐藏状态并在残差流中添加低秩修正，保持所有基础权重不变。在代码微调和偏好优化(DPO)任务上，LARA在相同参数数量下与LoRA匹配性能。由于适应是冻结基座加残差，LARA暴露推理时应用的尺度因子γ，可在基座行为和适应行为之间平滑插值。此外，每个行为是共享冻结基座上的小型残差模块，多个行为可同时驻留并按token自动路由——将七个行为存放在单个冻结1.5B模型上仅需约33MB开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的参数高效微调方法（如LoRA）在权重空间中进行适应，无法提供推理时的分级控制，且多行为托管需要加载不同的权重。LARA旨在通过在残差流中进行适应，实现基座与适应行为的平滑插值、多行为同时驻留和按token自动路由。

Method: LARA在冻结基础模型的少量层读取隐藏状态，向残差流添加低秩修正，而不修改任何基础权重。适应行为表示为一个共享冻结基座 + 残差模块的形式，暴露一个推理时尺度因子γ用于平滑插值。多个行为对应的残差模块可同时保存在模型中，并以token级别自动路由选择。

Result: 在代码微调和DPO任务上，LARA在相同参数数量下匹配LoRA的性能。将七个行为（六个微调、一个偏好优化）部署在一个冻结1.5B模型上仅需约33MB开销，相比每个行为需要单独一个完整模型显著节省资源。由于基础模型保持不变，各行为可单独训练并按token选择，无需按需加载。

Conclusion: LARA证明了在残差流中而非权重中进行适应是可行的，它保留了LoRA级别的高效性能，同时额外提供了分级控制（通过γ进行推理时平滑插值）和多行为驻留能力，特别适合在单一设备上托管和扩展多个行为。

Abstract: We present LARA (Lightweight Additive Residual Adaptation), a method for efficient adaptation that operates in the residual stream of a frozen model rather than in its weights. Where LoRA adds an update of low rank to weight matrices, LARA reads the hidden state at a small set of layers and adds a correction of low rank back to the residual stream, leaving all base weights untouched. On a code fine-tuning task and on preference optimization (DPO), LARA matches LoRA at equal parameter counts. Because adaptation is a frozen base plus a residual, LARA exposes a scale γ, applied at inference, that interpolates smoothly between base and adapted behavior, a form of graded control that adaptation in weight space does not offer. Finally, because each behavior is a small residual module over a shared frozen base, many behaviors can be held resident at once and routed automatically per token. We place seven behaviors, six fine-tuned and one optimized for preference, on one frozen 1.5B model for roughly 33 MB of overhead, against one full model for each behavior. Because the base is untouched, behaviors are trained separately and selected per token rather than loaded on demand, which suits hosting many behaviors, and adding new ones, on a single model on a device.

</details>


### [76] [Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-\texorpdfstring{$K$}{K} Routing: Two-Sided Dependence Control for Frozen Mixture-of-Experts at Fixed Per-Token Routing Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28670)
*Richard Yi Da Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种层次化Copula-Gumbel-Top-K（CGA）构造方法，在保持每个token的完整路由分布完全不变的前提下，通过Gaussian copula在同一组token内引入正相关耦合、通过负对偶构造在跨组间引入可控负相关，从而独立控制专家负载的方差（负载离散度）。该机制可附加于冻结的MoE模型之上，仅需一个小型控制器即可训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mixture-of-experts（MoE）模型中的Gumbel-Top-K路由为每个token定义一个路由律（ordered expert lists与mixture weights上的分布）。此前方法无法在固定单个token路由律的前提下，自由调节不同token在路由选择上的联合分布（如组内专家一致性vs.负载均衡）。作者旨在回答哪些joint分布可达，并用统一框架控制它们。

Method: 提出Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-K（CGA）两段式构造：（1）组内通过可交换Gaussian copula使各专家坐标上的Gumbel扰动正相关，增强组内专家集合的连贯性；（2）跨不相交组对之间通过可调的负对偶（antithetic）构造引入可控负相关。论文证明两种操作均保持每个token的有序Top-K采样、混合权重与包含概率在条件于pre-routing logits时与独立路由同分布，因此条件期望的专家流量不变。通过一个小型控制器在冻结特征上驱动依赖旋钮，使用score-function estimator训练，梯度仅落在控制器上。

Result: 理论刻画了权衡关系：组内正耦合只能增大专家负载的方差（相对独立路由），而跨组非负反对只能相对同组强度的平坦耦合降低方差。因此专家相干性与负载离散度由两个互补的依赖旋钮在不变约束曲面上控制。小规模初步实验验证了机制与训练路径，但未建立任务级微调收益。

Conclusion: CGA提供了一种不触碰基础模型、仅由小控制器调节的路由依赖机制，在保持个体路由律精确不变的同时，通过组内正相关与跨组负相关两个旋钮分别提升专家相干性和改善负载均衡。该工作为MoE路由的联合分布设计提供了理论框架，但任务级收益仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: A stochastic Gumbel-Top-$K$ router defines, for every token of a mixture-of-experts (MoE) model, a \emph{routing law}: a distribution over ordered expert lists and mixture weights. We ask which \emph{joint} distributions over the routing choices of different tokens are reachable while every individual token's complete routing law is held exactly fixed. We give a two-sided construction, \emph{Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-$K$} (\CGA{}). Within a group of related tokens, an exchangeable Gaussian copula positively correlates the Gumbel perturbations at each expert coordinate, which can increase within-group expert-set coherence. Across disjoint pairs of groups, a tunable antithetic construction introduces a selectable amount of negative dependence. We prove that both operations leave each token's ordered Top-$K$ sample, mixture weights, and inclusion probabilities identical in distribution to independent routing \emph{at a routing layer conditioned on its pre-routing logits}; conditional expected expert traffic is preserved as a consequence. We characterize the resulting trade-off: positive within-group coupling can only inflate the variance of realized expert loads relative to independent routing, while nonnegative cross-group opposition can only reduce it relative to flat coupling at the same within-group strength. Coherence and load dispersion are thus controlled by two complementary dependence dials on the invariance constraint surface. Because the base model is untouched, the dials can be driven by a small controller over frozen features, trainable with a score-function estimator: the frozen network is evaluated only in the forward direction, and gradients are confined to the controller. An initial small-scale pilot validates the mechanism and the training route, but does not establish task-level fine-tuning gains.

</details>


### [77] [LAWFUL: Law-Aligned Witness for Faithful Use of Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28672)
*Kevin Chen,Kenneth W. Parker,Anish Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAWFUL框架，用于检验神经网络是否真正学到并内部使用物理定律（如多普勒频率公式），通过覆盖感知的因果一致性度量、电路域有效性测试等四个可解释性缺口来验证模型的内部表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当神经网络能准确预测物理系统时，我们无法确定它是否将物理定律作为形式化、结构化的知识来使用；特别是对于连续变量的物理定律，缺乏系统性的方法来验证网络内部计算是否在整个定律有效域内使用该表示。

Method: 提出LAWFUL基础框架，识别并针对四个可解释性缺口：(1) 连续反事实的覆盖感知因果一致性度量；(2) 识别电路的域有效性测试；(3) 定律不变量与禁止行为的验证；(4) 推导物理量在电路中流动的量化。前两个缺口被完全解决，后两个缺口奠定基础。在Mocap2Radar transformer上验证多普勒频率定律。

Result: LAWFUL框架成功应用于Mocap2Radar transformer，验证该模型是否学习并内部使用多普勒频率定律f(t)=2v(t)/λ，其中训练数据中f(t)和v(t)均未直接出现，从而检验模型是否从数据中隐式发现物理定律。

Conclusion: LAWFUL为连续变量物理定律的可解释性验证提供了系统框架，填补了覆盖感知因果一致性测量和电路域有效性测试的空白，为验证神经网络内部是否真正使用物理定律奠定了基础。

Abstract: When a neural network predicts a physical system accurately, has it learned the governing law as formal, structured knowledge, and if so, does the network's internal computation actually use that representation throughout the law's domain of validity? We identify four interpretability gaps that limit answering these questions for {\em physics laws over continuous variables}: the absence of a coverage-aware causal-consistency measure over continuous counterfactuals; of a domain-of-validity test for the identified circuit; of a verification of the law's invariants and forbidden behaviors; and of a quantification of how a derived physical quantity flows through the circuit. We develop a foundational framework, LAWFUL, that closes the first two and lays groundwork for the remaining two, and illustrate it on the Mocap2Radar transformer, validating whether it learns and internally uses the Doppler frequency law $f(t) = \frac{2 v(t)}λ$ from motion-capture and radar data in which neither $f(t)$ nor $v(t)$ appears.

</details>


### [78] [MPP-GNN: Subject-Adaptive Community Detection for fMRI-Based Alzheimer's Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28681)
*Yang Zhang,Xiao Zhou,Jonathan Warrell,Avram Holmes,Xuan Zhang,Mark Gerstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种元概率池化图神经网络（MPP-GNN），用于克服现有基于GNN的fMRI脑网络分析方法中预设功能模块数量、忽视个体间差异以及模块发现未能指导连接模式学习的问题。MPP-GNN通过耦合的双层优化实现自适应的层次化图分区，发现受试者特异的功能模块，并以此为显式先验指导边细化与表示学习。在两个公开AD分类数据集上达到最高AUC，且发现的功能模块与Yeo脑图谱高度一致，并揭示了AD的网络级去分化模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的脑功能连接分析方法存在两个关键缺陷：一是预设所有被试的功能模块数量，忽略了个体间差异；二是发现的模块未被直接用于引导学习到的连接模式。这些问题限制了模型的分类性能和可解释性。

Method: 提出MPP-GNN框架，将模型任务建模为耦合的双层优化问题：通过层次化的自适应图分区发现受试者特异的功能模块，再将发现的脑模块作为显式先验来引导边细化和表示学习过程。

Result: 在两个公共AD分类数据集上，MPP-GNN相比已有的基线方法取得了最高的AUC；同时，分析表明模型发现的模块与Yeo脑图谱定义的典型功能网络组织显著对齐，并揭示出AD的网络级去分化模式。

Conclusion: MPP-GNN通过引入受试者特异的模块发现与模块先验引导机制，有效提升了脑疾病分类性能，并在神经科学上发现了有意义的AD相关去分化模式，兼具实用性与可解释性。

Abstract: Functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is a widely used technique for studying the brain. Recent methods that utilize graph neural networks (GNNs) for analysis of brain functional connectivity have shown great potential for the classification of brain disorders, such as Alzheimer's disease (AD). However, these methods often assume a preset number of functional modules across all subjects, which overlooks inter-subject variability. In addition, the discovered modules are rarely used to directly guide the learned connectivity patterns. Here, to address these issues, we propose a Meta Probabilistic Pooling GNN (MPP-GNN). We frame the model's task as a coupled, bilevel optimization that performs adaptive graph partitioning hierarchically to discover subject-specific modules and then uses the discovered brain modules as an explicit prior to guide edge refinement and representation learning. We validate MPP-GNN on two public datasets for AD classification, achieving the highest AUC in comparison to established baselines for both datasets. Furthermore, our analysis demonstrates that MPP-GNN shows significant alignment with the canonical functional-network organization defined by the Yeo brain atlas and reveals a network-level dedifferentiation pattern for AD.

</details>


### [79] [TerraNova: A Foundation Model for the Anthropocene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29527)
*Carlos Rodriguez-Pardo,Massimo Tavoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TerraNova，一个同时处理连续地球物理场与离散国家社会数据的基础模型，通过专用编码器、跨模态Transformer和超网络解码器，以对比学习目标统一双重视觉表征，实现了跨地缘-社会系统的联合建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类世的核心挑战在于将物理地球与人类社会建模为一个耦合系统，但现有的学习表示无法同时覆盖两者的观测范围。根本障碍在于几何不匹配：物理数据是连续场且忽略政治边界，而社会数据按行政单元统计。现有地球系统基础模型只服务前者，与后者耦合需损失性的边界平均。

Method: TerraNova在1,024个物理与社会记录的原生几何上训练，包括512个网格化地球系统场和512个国家指标。该方法包含：(1) 专用编码器分别表示位置、国家、时间和任务；(2) 跨模态Transformer将这些编码融合为共享时空状态；(3) 超网络为每次查询生成解码器，其证据头返回预测分布。模型采用两个对比学习目标：一是人口加权对齐每个国家与其领土内坐标，二是与携带图像语义的预训练地理空间嵌入对齐。

Result: 该表示在与专用地理空间编码器的竞争中具有竞争力，同时扩展了它们未覆盖的维度（时间、海洋和不确定性），并支持国家层面的能力。冻结的骨干网络能从稀疏观测中重建密集场，在消费级硬件上几分钟内适应未见变量。

Conclusion: TerraNova证明了通过匹配原生几何来统一地球物理与社会数据的可能性，为耦合人类-地球系统建模提供了一种可扩展的表示学习框架，且具备良好的泛化与适应能力。

Abstract: A defining problem of the Anthropocene is to model the physical Earth and human societies as one coupled system, yet no learned representation spans their observational breadth. We argue the obstacle is geometric: the physical Earth is measured as continuous fields that ignore political borders, whereas societies are reported for administrative units. Earth-system foundation models serve the first geometry; coupling it to the second has required lossy averaging over borders. We introduce TerraNova, a foundation model trained on 1,024 physical and societal records in their native geometries: 512 gridded Earth-system fields and 512 national indicators. Dedicated encoders represent location, country, time and task, cross-modal transformers fuse them into a shared spatiotemporal state, and a hypernetwork generates a per-query decoder whose evidential head returns a predictive distribution. Two contrastive objectives couple the representation: a population-weighted alignment between each country and coordinates in its territory, and one to pretrained geospatial embeddings carrying image-derived semantics. Read out through that decoder, the representation is competitive with purpose-built geospatial encoders while spanning axes they do not represent (time, oceans and uncertainty) and supporting country-level capabilities. The frozen backbone reconstructs dense fields from sparse observations and adapts to unseen variables in minutes on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [80] [Technological Advances in Detecting and Managing Cognitive Impairment in Older Adults: Trends, Challenges, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28687)
*Mohammad Asif,Azizuddin Khan,Mohd Azam,Anurag Rajkumar Bombarde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批判性综述了用于老年人认知障碍（从轻度认知障碍MCI到痴呆）检测与管理的多模态技术进展，涵盖EEG、神经影像、血液生物标志物与数字标记，并整合AI/ML/DL方法。文章提出跨学科分类法、方法论严谨性视角（强调受试者与站点无关的验证）、分层早期检测框架及对比表格，同时指出当前多数EEG深度模型基于小规模单中心数据，外推验证存疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人口老龄化，从轻度认知障碍向痴呆的认知衰退成为未来数十年的重大健康挑战，但常规评估常遗漏最早的征象，亟需系统整合新兴技术以提升早期检测与管理能力。

Method: 采用批判性综述方法，综合神经生理信号（EEG）、结构/分子神经影像（MRI、淀粉样/tau PET）、血液生物标志物及数字标记等多模态数据，经由AI/ML/DL集成分析；提出跨学科分类法、方法论严谨性视角（强调受试者与站点无关验证）、分层早期检测框架，以及检测方法、干预措施和风险/保护因素的对比表格。

Result: 研究显示EEG标记（α/θ变化、P300潜伏期）与深度模型（CNN、LSTM/BiLSTM、Transformer、自监督EEG基础模型）报告了较高准确率，但多基于小规模单中心数据，外推验证存疑。另一方面，血浆p-tau217已具备临床应用价值（2025年首个辅助阿尔茨海默病诊断的血液检测获批）；抗淀粉样疗法（lecanemab、donanemab）获批但疗效存在争议；多领域生活方式干预已成熟；可穿戴、远程、语音和VR工具实现连续生态有效监测，多模态融合提升了灵敏度和特异性。

Conclusion: 领域仍面临标准化、可解释性、数据隐私和公平外推部署等障碍，近期前景在于建立值得信赖的、多模态的、纵向验证的系统，将早期检测与可执行的个性化护理相连接。

Abstract: As populations age, cognitive decline from mild cognitive impairment (MCI) to dementia is a defining health challenge of the coming decades, yet routine assessment often misses its earliest signs. This article critically synthesizes recent technological advances for detecting and managing cognitive impairment in older adults, spanning neurophysiological signals (chiefly electroencephalography, EEG), structural and molecular neuroimaging (MRI and amyloid/tau PET), blood-based biomarkers, and digital markers, integrated through artificial intelligence (AI), machine learning (ML), and deep learning (DL). Beyond summarizing, it contributes a cross-disciplinary taxonomy, a methodological-rigor lens foregrounding subject- and site-independent validation, an integrative early-detection framework linking tiered screening to intervention, and comparison tables of detection methods, interventions, and risk and protective factors. EEG markers (alpha/theta changes, P300 latency) and deep models (CNNs, LSTM/BiLSTM, transformers, self-supervised EEG foundation models) report strong accuracy, yet many rest on small, single-site datasets unlikely to survive rigorous external validation. Elsewhere, gains are tangible: plasma p-tau217 has reached clinical utility, with the first blood test cleared to aid Alzheimer's diagnosis in 2025; anti-amyloid therapies (lecanemab, donanemab) are approved despite modest, contested benefits; and multidomain lifestyle prevention has matured. Wearable, remote, speech, and virtual-reality tools enable continuous, ecologically valid monitoring, and multimodal fusion improves sensitivity and specificity. Barriers remain: standardization, explainability, data privacy, and equitable, externally validated deployment. The field's near-term promise lies in trustworthy, multimodal, longitudinally validated systems linking early detection to actionable, personalized care.

</details>


### [81] [When Does On-Policy Interaction Help? Representational Tradeoffs in Value-Based Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29617)
*Luca Viano,Antoine Moulin,Audrey Huang,Volkan Cevher,Philip Amortila,Dylan J. Foster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了模仿学习（IL）中两种广泛使用但理解不足的干预手段——交互式专家查询和基于价值函数的策略生成——之间的相互作用。核心发现是：交互（on-policy querying）放宽了学习者的表示需求，只需能表示专家的价值函数即可，而无需表示专家策略本身。作者提出OVI算法，在专家价值函数可实现性假设下具有统计和计算效率，并通过负结果证明交互的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准行为克隆（BC）存在累积误差和性能瓶颈，尤其当学习者无法完美表示专家策略时（如蒸馏场景）。实践中已知两种干预手段（沿学习者自身轨迹交互式查询专家、使用价值函数估计生成策略而非直接拟合专家完整动作分布）能提升性能，但缺乏对这两种改进的本质及其相互作用的理论理解。

Method: 作者提出OVI（交互式、on-policy的模仿学习算法）：在专家的价值函数可实现（realizability）假设下，通过沿学习者自身轨迹交互查询专家并利用线性最大化预言机（linear maximization oracle）进行策略优化。同时给出理论分析，包括正命题（OVI的统计与计算效率）和负命题（证明在仅有专家价值函数可实现性假设时，离线IL算法必须牺牲与专家策略类复杂度相关的样本效率，即交互是必要条件）。

Result: 理论结果表明：交互式学习将表示需求从"专家策略可实现"放宽到"专家价值函数可实现"（后者通常更宽松）。负结果证明，没有交互时，离线IL需依赖专家策略类的复杂度。实验验证了这些发现：OVI在性能上优于离线策略类方法（BC）、交互式策略类方法（DAgger）以及离线基于价值函数的IL方法；当学习者网络表达能力明显弱于专家时优势最大。

Conclusion: 交互式专家查询之所以有效，是因为它降低了学习者的表示性要求——只需实现专家价值函数而无需实现专家策略。离线情景下这一灵活性不可达，因此交互是必要的。OVI算法在理论和实证两方面都验证了这种表示放松带来的性能优势。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL)---training an agent to replicate expert behavior from demonstrations---underpins applications from robotics to language model training. Standard approaches such as Behavior Cloning (BC) are known to suffer from compounding errors and performance plateaus, particularly when the learner cannot perfectly represent the expert's policy (as is typical, e.g., in distillation). Two interventions are widely understood empirically to improve performance: querying the expert interactively along the learner's own trajectories, and using value function estimation en route to generating a policy rather than directly fitting the expert's full action distribution.
  We investigate the nature of these improvements and their potentially surprising interplay. Our main finding is that expert interaction relaxes the representational demands on the learner: one only needs a model capable of realizing the expert's value function, bypassing the (often stricter) requirement of realizing the expert's policy itself. Concretely, we introduce OVI, an interactive on-policy IL algorithm that is statistically efficient whenever the learner can represent the expert's value function and computationally efficient given access to a linear maximization oracle. We complement this with a negative result showing that interaction is necessary. Namely, without stronger assumptions beyond expert-value realizability alone, any offline IL algorithm must scale with the complexity of the expert policy class. Our findings bear out empirically. OVI outperforms offline policy-based (BC), interactive policy-based (DAgger), and offline value-based IL methods, with the largest gains when the learner network is substantially less expressive than the expert's.

</details>


### [82] [SEDR-Seq2P: A Lightweight Dilated Residual Sequence-to-Point Network for Multi-Task Industrial NILM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28693)
*Hatem Haddad,Feres Jerbi,Issam Smaali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SEDR-Seq2P，一种轻量级Seq2Point扩展模型，通过空洞残差块与挤压-激励注意力解决工业非侵入式负荷监测（NILM）中测量噪声和多设备并发运行带来的泛化问题，在高精度下显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业NILM仍具挑战性：测量噪声和广泛的多机器并发运行使针对住宅数据调优的模型泛化能力下降，需要一种兼顾精度与效率的多任务分解方案。

Method: 采用一对多多任务分解设置，单个网络同时估计多个工业设备负荷；在IMDELD统一评估协议下对比Seq2Seq、Seq2SubSeq、Seq2Point、GRU和WaveNet，并提出SEDR-Seq2P——在Seq2Point基础上加入空洞残差块和挤压-激励注意力机制的轻量级扩展。

Result: SEDR-Seq2P相较Seq2Point基线MAE降低约7%，决定系数提升约1%，匹配率提升约0.8%；相较WaveNet推理延迟降低约58%，在精度与延迟间取得更优平衡。

Conclusion: SEDR-Seq2P实现了精度-延迟的有利权衡，适合可扩展的工业部署场景。

Abstract: Industrial NILM remains challenging because measurement noise and widespread concurrent machine operation reduce the generalization of models tuned on residential data. This work adopts a one-to-many, multi-task disaggregation setting, in which a single network estimates multiple industrial machine loads from aggregate power. Under a unified evaluation protocol on IMDELD, we benchmark Seq2Seq, Seq2SubSeq, Seq2Point, GRU, and WaveNet using energy-estimation metrics and the accuracy-delay criterion. While Seq2Point offers a stronger accuracy-delay balance than Seq2Seq/Seq2SubSeq, GRU and WaveNet achieve higher accuracy at markedly higher computational cost. To close this gap, we propose SEDR-Seq2P, a lightweight Seq2Point extension with dilated residual blocks and squeeze-and-excitation attention. Relative to the Seq2Point baseline, SEDR-Seq2P reduces MAE by approximately 7%, improves the coefficient of determination by approximately 1%, and increases the match rate by approximately 0.8%.
